aḍḍhapaṇṇāsako: {group of twenty-five, half a fifty (nominative): पन्नास चर्चांचा छोटा अर्धा गट}
Ujjotanā: {shedding light upon (nominative): उजळून टाकणारा}
bhāṇavāraṃ: {portions for recitation (accusative): पठणाचा भाग}
Ekādhippāyena: {by a common purpose, heading (instrumental): सामायिक हेतू}
Pañcattiṃsa: {the number 35 (numeral): पस्तीस}
sambuddha: {fully enlightened (compound member): जागृत बुद्ध}
namo: {reverence, salutation (indeclinable): नमन}
